1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a stationarity algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 501. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 240/3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 501 + O(1/n^240). Summing over all modes and applying the stationarity convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.5 (Spectral Property)
